- **financial-statement-tie-out**: Use when asked to tie out, reconcile, or sanity-check financial statements or a close's intake — the Balance Sheet balances every month, Current Year Earnings equals year-to-date net income, subtotals foot to their lines, and gross profit = revenue − COGS.
